FloodGuard · Evidence and scenario report

Package 8ad2652d399e73aa · generated 2026-09-21T08:35:05+00:00

Non-operational research demonstration. Historical observations, candidate estimates and imposed scenarios do not establish event-specific flood accuracy, actual shelter availability or observed road passability.

363 source files verified; 17 acquired dataset selections across six AOIs and eight event packages. Raw files remain outside the hosted export. Gauge observations and unresolved-rights geometries are available only in the local research package. No private agency export is required.

Additional public website acquisitions

NGIS/DWR natural streams and water bodies were downloaded as full features intersecting each AOI rectangle. Counts overlap between AOIs. Observation epoch and redistribution terms remain unresolved, so geometry stays local and is not an event flood mask or a complete drainage inventory.

aoi-01_mae_sai_core_mae_sai_2024

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • The preserved /studio/ satellite comparator uses transformed uncalibrated amplitude and cross-border weak-reference metrics. Those metrics do not measure Thailand-side event accuracy and are not FPPS inputs here.
  • WorldPop 2020 is modelled residential population context, not event-year counts or observed evacuation demand.
  • Demand contains positive raster cells whose centres fall in the AOI; boundary cells are not apportioned.
  • Routing uses a surrounding district/basin polygon; paths outside that polygon are not evaluated.
  • Road times use fixed class speeds on an undirected graph; one-way rules, turn restrictions and event passability are not represented.
  • Only shared original OSM vertex coordinates with compatible layer/bridge/tunnel tags connect. Geometric crossings are never noded.
  • Conservative grade separation may disconnect bridge approaches; disconnected components remain visible.
  • 100 m facility and 250 m population snaps use EPSG:32647; connector time is modelled at 5 km/h. Connector walkability and barriers are unverified.
  • OSM healthcare points are candidate destinations. Generic amenity=shelter may be a bus shelter, farm cottage or rest pavilion and is excluded from destination access and shelter capacity.
  • No flood layer is converted into observed closures or calibrated flood probability.
  • Destination source for this scenario family is explicitly osm.
  • dataset-01 — Analysis coverage 81.0%; outside footprint unobserved. No accepted September-only reference.
  • dataset-02 — Analysis coverage 81.0%; outside footprint unobserved. No accepted September-only reference.
  • dataset-06 — 2018–2023; ปี 2561–2566
  • dataset-07 — 2025; ปี 2568; not observed 2024 ages
  • dataset-08 — 2022–2025
  • dataset-12 — 13 source rows at 4 distinct coordinates; identity/activation not established.
  • dataset-13 — 3 historical 2020 points within the search AOI; event availability unknown.
  • dataset-14 — 118420 valid pixels; 0 negative elevations retained for review; min/max 375.40/1308.16 m.
  • dataset-15 — Version 1; generalized historical network
  • dataset-16 — 11 September 2024
  • context-osm — 42737 modelled edges; 203 disconnected graph components. Paths beyond the routing boundary are unevaluated. 23/27 mapped sites satisfy the 100 m connector limit; generic shelters are context only.
  • context-worldpop — 2020 modelled population 44166.63; 96.74% within 250 m. 3166 nodata/invalid cells have unknown population, separate from known unsnapped demand.

aoi-02_mae_sai_district_mae_sai_2024

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • The preserved /studio/ satellite comparator uses transformed uncalibrated amplitude and cross-border weak-reference metrics. Those metrics do not measure Thailand-side event accuracy and are not FPPS inputs here.
  • Surrounding district/basin AOI supplies routing context for the corresponding core demonstration. This package presents evidence coverage and unresolved gaps; no district/basin access result is claimed.
  • dataset-01 — Analysis coverage 81.4%; outside footprint unobserved. No accepted September-only reference.
  • dataset-02 — Analysis coverage 81.4%; outside footprint unobserved. No accepted September-only reference.

aoi-03_hat_yai_core_hat_yai_2025

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • Public metadata identifies the November 11/23 Sentinel-1 pair, but original SAFE archives were not acquired. These imposed access/capacity experiments are the explicit scenario fallback, not a satellite flood candidate.
  • WorldPop 2020 is modelled residential population context, not event-year counts or observed evacuation demand.
  • Demand contains positive raster cells whose centres fall in the AOI; boundary cells are not apportioned.
  • Routing uses a surrounding district/basin polygon; paths outside that polygon are not evaluated.
  • Road times use fixed class speeds on an undirected graph; one-way rules, turn restrictions and event passability are not represented.
  • Only shared original OSM vertex coordinates with compatible layer/bridge/tunnel tags connect. Geometric crossings are never noded.
  • Conservative grade separation may disconnect bridge approaches; disconnected components remain visible.
  • 100 m facility and 250 m population snaps use EPSG:32647; connector time is modelled at 5 km/h. Connector walkability and barriers are unverified.
  • OSM healthcare points are candidate destinations. Generic amenity=shelter may be a bus shelter, farm cottage or rest pavilion and is excluded from destination access and shelter capacity.
  • No flood layer is converted into observed closures or calibrated flood probability.
  • Destination source for this scenario family is explicitly osm.
  • dataset-05 — Station-month observations include missing measurements; absent timestamps and present null/sentinel readings are checked separately. Observation timezone remains unconfirmed.
  • dataset-10 — 2019–2025; district and sex, no age bands
  • dataset-11 — 2023–2025; village registration context
  • dataset-12 — 21 source rows at 20 distinct coordinates; identity/activation not established.
  • dataset-13 — 4 historical 2020 points within the search AOI; event availability unknown.
  • dataset-14 — 105625 valid pixels; 4 negative elevations retained for review; min/max -0.63/382.84 m.
  • dataset-15 — Version 1; generalized historical network
  • dataset-17 — 27 November 2025
  • context-osm — 96755 modelled edges; 559 disconnected graph components. Paths beyond the routing boundary are unevaluated. 48/48 mapped sites satisfy the 100 m connector limit; generic shelters are context only.
  • context-worldpop — 2020 modelled population 264044.87; 99.35% within 250 m. 24 nodata/invalid cells have unknown population, separate from known unsnapped demand.

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • Public metadata identifies the November 11/23 Sentinel-1 pair, but original SAFE archives were not acquired. These imposed access/capacity experiments are the explicit scenario fallback, not a satellite flood candidate.
  • Surrounding district/basin AOI supplies routing context for the corresponding core demonstration. This package presents evidence coverage and unresolved gaps; no district/basin access result is claimed.
  • dataset-05 — Station-month observations include missing measurements; absent timestamps and present null/sentinel readings are checked separately. Observation timezone remains unconfirmed.
  • dataset-10 — 2019–2025; district and sex, no age bands
  • dataset-11 — 2023–2025; village registration context
  • dataset-12 — 72 source rows at 64 distinct coordinates; identity/activation not established.
  • dataset-13 — 21 historical 2020 points within the search AOI; event availability unknown.
  • dataset-14 — 571753 valid pixels; 22 negative elevations retained for review; min/max -1.62/387.31 m.
  • dataset-15 — Version 1; generalized historical network
  • dataset-17 — 27 November 2025

aoi-05_chao_phraya_bang_ban_sena_chao_phraya_2024

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • The 2024 and 2025 packages reuse the same historical OSM/WorldPop context and imposed scenarios. Their event evidence inventories differ; scenario differences between observed flood years are not claimed.
  • WorldPop 2020 is modelled residential population context, not event-year counts or observed evacuation demand.
  • Demand contains positive raster cells whose centres fall in the AOI; boundary cells are not apportioned.
  • Routing uses a surrounding district/basin polygon; paths outside that polygon are not evaluated.
  • Road times use fixed class speeds on an undirected graph; one-way rules, turn restrictions and event passability are not represented.
  • Only shared original OSM vertex coordinates with compatible layer/bridge/tunnel tags connect. Geometric crossings are never noded.
  • Conservative grade separation may disconnect bridge approaches; disconnected components remain visible.
  • 100 m facility and 250 m population snaps use EPSG:32647; connector time is modelled at 5 km/h. Connector walkability and barriers are unverified.
  • OSM healthcare points are candidate destinations. Generic amenity=shelter may be a bus shelter, farm cottage or rest pavilion and is excluded from destination access and shelter capacity.
  • No flood layer is converted into observed closures or calibrated flood probability.
  • Destination source for this scenario family is explicitly osm.
  • dataset-03 — Station-month observations include missing measurements; absent timestamps and present null/sentinel readings are checked separately. Observation timezone remains unconfirmed.
  • dataset-12 — 1 source rows at 1 distinct coordinates; identity/activation not established.
  • dataset-13 — 16 historical 2020 points within the search AOI; event availability unknown.
  • dataset-14 — 187489 valid pixels; 244 negative elevations retained for review; min/max -4.93/22.92 m.
  • dataset-15 — Version 1; generalized historical network
  • context-osm — 33259 modelled edges; 361 disconnected graph components. Paths beyond the routing boundary are unevaluated. 1/1 mapped sites satisfy the 100 m connector limit; generic shelters are context only.
  • context-worldpop — 2020 modelled population 50467.26; 93.30% within 250 m. 59 nodata/invalid cells have unknown population, separate from known unsnapped demand.

aoi-05_chao_phraya_bang_ban_sena_chao_phraya_2025

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • The 2024 and 2025 packages reuse the same historical OSM/WorldPop context and imposed scenarios. Their event evidence inventories differ; scenario differences between observed flood years are not claimed.
  • WorldPop 2020 is modelled residential population context, not event-year counts or observed evacuation demand.
  • Demand contains positive raster cells whose centres fall in the AOI; boundary cells are not apportioned.
  • Routing uses a surrounding district/basin polygon; paths outside that polygon are not evaluated.
  • Road times use fixed class speeds on an undirected graph; one-way rules, turn restrictions and event passability are not represented.
  • Only shared original OSM vertex coordinates with compatible layer/bridge/tunnel tags connect. Geometric crossings are never noded.
  • Conservative grade separation may disconnect bridge approaches; disconnected components remain visible.
  • 100 m facility and 250 m population snaps use EPSG:32647; connector time is modelled at 5 km/h. Connector walkability and barriers are unverified.
  • OSM healthcare points are candidate destinations. Generic amenity=shelter may be a bus shelter, farm cottage or rest pavilion and is excluded from destination access and shelter capacity.
  • No flood layer is converted into observed closures or calibrated flood probability.
  • Destination source for this scenario family is explicitly osm.
  • dataset-04 — Station-month observations include missing measurements; absent timestamps and present null/sentinel readings are checked separately. Observation timezone remains unconfirmed.
  • dataset-12 — 1 source rows at 1 distinct coordinates; identity/activation not established.
  • dataset-13 — 16 historical 2020 points within the search AOI; event availability unknown.
  • dataset-14 — 187489 valid pixels; 244 negative elevations retained for review; min/max -4.93/22.92 m.
  • dataset-15 — Version 1; generalized historical network
  • context-osm — 33259 modelled edges; 361 disconnected graph components. Paths beyond the routing boundary are unevaluated. 1/1 mapped sites satisfy the 100 m connector limit; generic shelters are context only.
  • context-worldpop — 2020 modelled population 50467.26; 93.30% within 250 m. 59 nodata/invalid cells have unknown population, separate from known unsnapped demand.

aoi-06_chao_phraya_rangsit_chao_phraya_2024

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • The 2024 and 2025 packages reuse the same historical OSM/WorldPop context and imposed scenarios. Their event evidence inventories differ; scenario differences between observed flood years are not claimed.
  • WorldPop 2020 is modelled residential population context, not event-year counts or observed evacuation demand.
  • Demand contains positive raster cells whose centres fall in the AOI; boundary cells are not apportioned.
  • Routing uses a surrounding district/basin polygon; paths outside that polygon are not evaluated.
  • Road times use fixed class speeds on an undirected graph; one-way rules, turn restrictions and event passability are not represented.
  • Only shared original OSM vertex coordinates with compatible layer/bridge/tunnel tags connect. Geometric crossings are never noded.
  • Conservative grade separation may disconnect bridge approaches; disconnected components remain visible.
  • 100 m facility and 250 m population snaps use EPSG:32647; connector time is modelled at 5 km/h. Connector walkability and barriers are unverified.
  • OSM healthcare points are candidate destinations. Generic amenity=shelter may be a bus shelter, farm cottage or rest pavilion and is excluded from destination access and shelter capacity.
  • No flood layer is converted into observed closures or calibrated flood probability.
  • Destination source for this scenario family is explicitly osm.
  • dataset-03 — Station-month observations include missing measurements; absent timestamps and present null/sentinel readings are checked separately. Observation timezone remains unconfirmed.
  • dataset-09 — 2018–2024; provincial age bands
  • dataset-12 — 14 source rows at 14 distinct coordinates; identity/activation not established.
  • dataset-13 — 5 historical 2020 points within the search AOI; event availability unknown.
  • dataset-14 — 156313 valid pixels; 133 negative elevations retained for review; min/max -13.75/26.54 m.
  • dataset-15 — Version 1; generalized historical network
  • context-osm — 56186 modelled edges; 619 disconnected graph components. Paths beyond the routing boundary are unevaluated. 17/17 mapped sites satisfy the 100 m connector limit; generic shelters are context only.
  • context-worldpop — 2020 modelled population 564292.56; 99.51% within 250 m. 197 nodata/invalid cells have unknown population, separate from known unsnapped demand.

aoi-06_chao_phraya_rangsit_chao_phraya_2025

Actual FPPS and action class: unavailable. Fixed-weight sensitivity range 0–100; no qualified event input is silently filled.

  • Mixed historical source dates are recorded per dataset; no common event observation timestamp.
  • Search AOIs are not administrative reporting boundaries.
  • Candidate/scenario outputs are not an official warning.
  • The 2024 and 2025 packages reuse the same historical OSM/WorldPop context and imposed scenarios. Their event evidence inventories differ; scenario differences between observed flood years are not claimed.
  • WorldPop 2020 is modelled residential population context, not event-year counts or observed evacuation demand.
  • Demand contains positive raster cells whose centres fall in the AOI; boundary cells are not apportioned.
  • Routing uses a surrounding district/basin polygon; paths outside that polygon are not evaluated.
  • Road times use fixed class speeds on an undirected graph; one-way rules, turn restrictions and event passability are not represented.
  • Only shared original OSM vertex coordinates with compatible layer/bridge/tunnel tags connect. Geometric crossings are never noded.
  • Conservative grade separation may disconnect bridge approaches; disconnected components remain visible.
  • 100 m facility and 250 m population snaps use EPSG:32647; connector time is modelled at 5 km/h. Connector walkability and barriers are unverified.
  • OSM healthcare points are candidate destinations. Generic amenity=shelter may be a bus shelter, farm cottage or rest pavilion and is excluded from destination access and shelter capacity.
  • No flood layer is converted into observed closures or calibrated flood probability.
  • Destination source for this scenario family is explicitly osm.
  • dataset-04 — Station-month observations include missing measurements; absent timestamps and present null/sentinel readings are checked separately. Observation timezone remains unconfirmed.
  • dataset-09 — 2018–2024; provincial age bands
  • dataset-12 — 14 source rows at 14 distinct coordinates; identity/activation not established.
  • dataset-13 — 5 historical 2020 points within the search AOI; event availability unknown.
  • dataset-14 — 156313 valid pixels; 133 negative elevations retained for review; min/max -13.75/26.54 m.
  • dataset-15 — Version 1; generalized historical network
  • context-osm — 56186 modelled edges; 619 disconnected graph components. Paths beyond the routing boundary are unevaluated. 17/17 mapped sites satisfy the 100 m connector limit; generic shelters are context only.
  • context-worldpop — 2020 modelled population 564292.56; 99.51% within 250 m. 197 nodata/invalid cells have unknown population, separate from known unsnapped demand.
